People keep telling me it's just staffing shortages, but... work I’ve caught myself rocking in the hallway, copying a patient’s tapping, or standing there with my gloves half on like I’m stuck, when I know I’m supposed to be doing rounds.\n- key: S024\n  name: manifestation: I used to sketch murals, cook for my friends, and play soccer on weekends, but now all of that feels pointless and I usually just ignore messages and stay in my apartment.\n- key:
